\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=|\alpha|-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}||\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}||\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}|dvdx

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}||\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}||\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}|dvdx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\nabla^{N}_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

By a similar way, note that
\left(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{v}f,\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)=0,
we can deduce that

(3.6)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\left\|\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\right\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

As for the third term on the right-hand side of (
3.1
), by using macro-micro decomposition, we have

(3.7)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

where we use the fact that

\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)=0,

due to the kernel structure of
{\bf P}
and the integral of oddness function with respect to velocity
v
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
.

Using various Sobolev inequalities and Cauchy inequality, we can get that the first two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.7
) can be controlled by

(3.8)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\frac{\eta}{{\varepsilon^{2}}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D},

as for the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.7
), by using a similar way as the estimates on
\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(q_{0}(v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(q_{0}(v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{4}},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=N-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\|B_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N-2}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

Now we arrive at

(3.9)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(q_{0}(v\times B_{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right),\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=N-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\|B_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N-2}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

By using Lemma
A.2
and Cauchy inequality, one can get that the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.7
) can be dominated by

\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\frac{\eta}{{\varepsilon^{2}}}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}{\{\bf I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

Now we arrive at by collecting the above estimates

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left(\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\nabla^{N}_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=N-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N-2}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

By plugging (
3.4
) and (
3.1
) into (
3.1
), one has (
3.1
).
Thus the proof of Lemma
3.1
is complete.
∎

Lemma 3.2
.

There exists an interactive energy functional
\mathcal{E}^{int}_{N}(t)
satisfying

\mathcal{E}^{int}_{N}(t)\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}

such that

(3.10)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}^{int}_{N}(t)+\left\|\nabla_{x}[\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\pm},u_{\varepsilon},\theta_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N-1}_{x}}+\|\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-}\|^{2}+\|E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N-1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N-2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

Proof.

This lemma can be proved by macroscopically projecting the original equation. For the sake of brevity in exposition, the details of the proof are provided in Appendix
A.3
.
∎

Assumption 1:

(3.11)

\displaystyle\sup_{0<t\leq T}\left\{(1+t)^{\varrho+\frac{3}{2}}\left\|E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+(1+t)^{\varrho+\frac{5}{2}}\left\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}\leq M_{1}

where
M_{1}
is a sufficiently small positive constant.

Proposition 3.1
.

Under
Assumption 1
,
there exist an energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)
and the corresponding energy dissipation functional
\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)
which satisfy (
2.1
), (
2.2
) respectively such that

(3.12)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}(1+t)^{-\varrho-\frac{3}{2}}\left\{\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\nabla^{N}_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=N-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq N-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

A proper linear combination of (
3.1
) and (
3.10
) gives (
3.12
).
∎

3.2.
The top-order energy estimates with weight

To control the first term on the right-hand side of (
3.12
), one need the energy estimate with the weight. However, To overcome this difficulty term, we have the following the result:

Lemma 3.3
.

It holds that

(3.13)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\vartheta q{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)

for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

For this purpose, the standard energy estimate on
\partial^{\alpha}f
with
|\alpha|=N
weighted by the time-velocity dependent function
w_{l}(\alpha,0)(t,v)
gives

(3.14)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\vartheta q}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot vf_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

where the coercivity estimates on
\mathscr{L}
i.e. (
A.2
) tell us that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\mathscr{L}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

To control the singularity
\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}
in
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}
on the right-hand side of (
3.14
), we have to multiply
{\varepsilon^{2}}
to (
3.14
) such that

(3.15)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\vartheta q{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot vf_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|.

By using Sobolev inequalities, one has

(3.16)

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot vf_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha_{1}|=N-1\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha_{1}|=N-1\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{6}_{x}}

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\eta\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

where we used the fact that

\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\lesssim w_{l}(\alpha-\alpha_{1})\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}-4|\alpha_{1}|}\lesssim w_{l}(\alpha-\alpha_{1})\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}.

As for
{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|
, one need to estimate it very carefully because of lack of
\varepsilon-
order,

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\eta\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

where the last term can be bounded by

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}\left|\left([v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\varepsilon}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\varepsilon}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\xi||\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[w_{l}(\alpha-\alpha_{1})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}-4|\alpha_{1}|}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\times\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right]\right|d\xi dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\xi||\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[w_{l}(\alpha-\alpha_{1})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle+\eta{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\xi|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

Similarly, one also has

(3.17)

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}M_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}M_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{1/2}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

where we use
Assumption 1
and take

0<\vartheta\leq\frac{\varrho}{2}-\frac{1}{4}.

The last term on the right-hand side of (
3.15
) can be bounded by

(3.18)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\varepsilon}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

By collecting the related inequalities into (
3.15
), one has (
3.13
),
which complete the proof of Lemma
3.3
.
∎

In addition to the above highest-order energy estimates with the wight
w(\alpha,0)
for
|\alpha|=N
, to avoid the macroscopic part with the singularity factor
\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}
, for the other cases with weight, we applying the micro projection equality.

Lemma 3.4
.

It holds that

(3.19)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\alpha|\leq N-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=N-1,|\beta|=1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

Proof.

To this end, by applying the microscopic projection
\{{\bf I-P}\}
to the first equation of (
1.12
), we can get that

(3.20)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}v\cdot E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}+q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}).

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
into (
3.20
), multiplying
w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}
and integrating the result identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, then we have

(3.21)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}v\cdot E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}+q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

The coercivity estimates on the linear operator
\mathscr{L}
i.e. (
A.10
) yields that

(3.22)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}.

As for the transport term on the right-hand side of (
3.21
), one has

(3.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}dvdx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{e_{j}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}dvdx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}i\xi_{j}\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\overline{\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]}d\xi dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dx

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\overline{\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]}\right|^{2}d\xi dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dx

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\overline{\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta-e_{j})\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]}\right|^{2}d\xi dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta-e_{j})\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

Here we used the fact

\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)=\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta-e_{j})\langle v\rangle^{-4}\leq\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta-e_{j}).

For the second and third term on the right-hand side of (
3.21
), it is straightford to compute that

(3.24)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

and

(3.25)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{D}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

As for the fourth term on the right-hand side of (
3.21
), one has

(3.26)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

Applying macroscopic part and the Cauchy inequality, one can deduce that

(3.27)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
3.26
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\right]\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\left[\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right),

when
|\alpha_{1}|=1,\beta_{1}=0
,
we apply the Parseval identity to get

(3.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi||\partial^{e_{i}}B_{\varepsilon}|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[v_{j}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\times\left|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|d\xi dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi||\partial^{e_{i}}B_{\varepsilon}|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[v_{j}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{e_{i}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha-e_{i},\beta)\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}}|\xi|\left|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\mathcal{F}_{v}\left[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right|^{2}d\xi dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}

where we used the fact that

v_{j}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\leq v_{j}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{5}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha-e_{i},\beta)\leq\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha-e_{i},\beta).

The other cases have similar upper bound by utilizing a similar way. Thus, one has

(3.29)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

By using the similar argument as the estimate on (
3.26
),
one has

(3.30)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}v\cdot E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}+q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right],w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

By using (
A.4
)-(
A.5
),
one can deduce that

(3.31)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)++\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{D}.

Now by collecting the above related estimates into (
3.21
), we arrive at

(3.32)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

The other cases can be dominated by the same upper bound as (
3.32
).
A proper linear combination (
3.32
) with
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N,|\alpha|\leq N-1
implies (
3.19
).
∎

A proper linear combination of Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
3.4
gives that

Proposition 3.2
.

It holds that

(3.33)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\sum_{|\alpha|=N}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\vartheta q{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

3.3.
The low-order energy estimates with weight

By utilizing a slightly different technique as Proposition
3.2
, we also have

Proposition 3.3
.

It holds that

(3.34)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

3.4.
Lyapunov inequality for the energy functionals

Proposition 3.4
.

Under
Assumption 1
,
take
l\geq N+\frac{1}{2}
, we can deduce that

(3.35)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim 0

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
, (
3.12
) tells us that

(3.36)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}(1+t)^{-(1+\epsilon_{0})}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\nabla^{N}_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\nabla^{N-1}_{x}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq N-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{7}{4}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

where we ask that
l\geq N+\frac{1}{2}
.

By multiplying
(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}
into (
3.36
), one has

(3.37)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}+\epsilon_{0}(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

By multiplying
(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}
into (
3.33
), one has

(3.38)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\{\sum_{|\alpha|=N}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|w_{\ell}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\frac{\vartheta q{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N}\left\|w_{\ell}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N}\|w_{\ell}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

Thus (
3.35
) follows from (
3.36
), (
3.37
), (
3.38
) and (
3.34
), which complete the proof of this proposition.
∎

3.5.
The temporal time decay estimate on
\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)

To ensure
Assumption
1 in (
3.11
), this subsection is devoted into the temporal time decay rates for
[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]
to
the Cauchy problem (
1.12
).

Assumption 2:

\sup_{0<t\leq T}\left\{\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\right\}\leq M_{2},

where
M_{2}
is a sufficiently small positive constant.

Lemma 3.5
.

Under
Assumption 1
and
Assumption 2
, there exists a suitably large constant
\bar{l}
, and take
l\geq\bar{l}
,
\widetilde{k}=\min\{k+1,N_{0}-1\}
let
N_{0}\geq 4
,
N=2N_{0}
, one has the following estimates:

(i).

For
k=0,1,\cdots,N_{0}-1
, it holds that

(3.39)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\nabla^{k}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right)+\eta\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

(iii).

For
k=N_{0}
, it follows that

(3.40)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}-1}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}\right)+\eta\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}.

(iii).

For
k=0,1,2\cdots,N_{0}-1
, there exist interactive energy functionals
G^{k}_{f}(t)
satisfying

G^{k}_{f}(t)\lesssim\left\|\nabla^{k}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+1}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+2}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

such that

(3.41)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}G^{k}_{f}(t)+\left\|\nabla^{k}[E_{\varepsilon},\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho^{-}_{{}_{\varepsilon}}]\right\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+1}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}])\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)+\frac{1}{{\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{D}}.

Proof.

We can use a similar approach to Lemma
4.3
to prove this lemma. For brevity, we omit its detailed proof.
∎

Proposition 3.5
.

Under
Assumption 1
and
Assumption 2
,
there exist an energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)
and the corresponding energy dissipation rate functional
\mathcal{D}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)
satisfying (
2.6
) and (
2.7
) respectively such that

(3.42)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\leq 0

holds for
k=0,1,2,\cdots,N_{0}-2
and all
0\leq t\leq T.

Furthermore, we can get that

(3.43)

\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)},\quad 0\leq t\leq T.

Proof.

(
3.42
) follows from (
3.39
), (
3.40
) and (
3.41
). To deduce (
3.43
),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{k}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|\leq\left\|\nabla^{k+1}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}.

The above inequality together with the facts that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{m}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{m}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{(\gamma+2){k+\frac{1}{2}}}{2}}\nabla^{m}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}},

(3.44)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}-1}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}+k+\frac{1}{2}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}

imply

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\leq\left(\mathcal{D}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\right)^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\{\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}.

Hence, we deduce that

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)+\left\{\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\right\}^{1+\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\leq 0

and we can get by solving the above inequality directly that

\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim\max\{M_{1},M_{2}\}(1+t)^{-{k+\varrho}}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.5
.
∎

3.6.
The estimates on the negative Sobolev space

To ensure
Assumption
2, this subsection is devoted into
bound on
\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}](t)\right\|
.

The first one is on the estimate on
\|[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}](t)\|_{\dot{H}^{-\varrho}}
.

Lemma 3.6
.

It holds that

(3.45)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{1}_{v}}^{2}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}H^{3}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}^{2}.

Proof.

By taking Fourier transform of the first equation of (
1.12
) with respect to
x
, multiplying the resulting identity by
|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\bar{\hat{f}}_{\pm}
with
\bar{\hat{f}}_{\pm}
being the complex conjugate of
\hat{f}_{\pm}
, and integrating the final result with respect to
\xi
and
v
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
that
we can get by using the coercivity property
\mathscr{L}

(3.46)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left(v\cdot\mathcal{F}[q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{\mathscr{T}}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon})]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|.

One has by macro-micro decomposition

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot vf_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\right)+\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}[{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\right)+\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}[{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\right).

The first three terms on the right-hand side of the above equation can be bounded by

(3.47)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot{M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left({M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|\lesssim\left\|E_{\varepsilon}\cdot{M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left({M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|{M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left({M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\left({M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left({M}^{\delta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right).

As for the last term, one has

(3.48)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}[{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left({\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right\|_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{3}{\varrho}}}\left\|{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|\right)^{2}+\eta\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

Consequently, one has

(3.49)

\displaystyle\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot vf_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

Similarly, one has

(3.50)

\displaystyle\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

For the third term on the right-hand side of (
3.46
), we have by repeating the argument used in deducing the estimate on the first two terms that

(3.51)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf P}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf P}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf P}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

where we used the fact that

\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf P}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|=0.

Similar with the argument used in (
3.47
), one has

(3.52)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf P}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

By using the similar way as (
3.48
), one has

(3.53)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D},

consequently, one has

(3.54)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\left(\mathcal{F}[{q_{0}}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\hat{f}_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left(\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|f\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|+\left\|f\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\right\|^{2}\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

As to the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.46
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\mathcal{F}[{\mathscr{T}}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon})]\mid|\xi|^{-2\varrho}\mathcal{F}\left[{{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathscr{T}}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon})\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathscr{T}}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon})\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right|_{H^{3}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right|_{H^{1}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\right\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}H^{3}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}.

Substituting the estimates into (
3.46
) yields (
3.45
), which complete the proof of Lemma
3.6
.
∎

Lemma 3.7
.

There exists an interactive functional
G^{-\varrho}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G^{-\varrho}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

such that

(3.55)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}G^{-\varrho}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-})\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{D}}+\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)\mathcal{D}_{2}(t)

holds for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

This lemma can be proved by a similar way as Lemma
3.2
, we omit its proof for brevity.
∎

Based on the above two lemmas, one has

Proposition 3.6
.

Under Under
Assumption 1
and
Assumption 2
, one has

(3.56)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}G^{-\varrho}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-})\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(M_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+M_{2}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla_{x}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{1}_{s+\frac{\gamma}{2}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{{D}}+\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)\mathcal{D}_{2}(t).

Proof.

By the interpolation with respect to the spatial derivatives, one has

(3.57)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{D}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D},

and for
\frac{1}{2}<\varrho<\frac{3}{2}
, then
\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}>1-\varrho
,

\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\lesssim\eta\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+C_{\eta}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2},

Similarly,

\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\lesssim\eta\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+C_{\eta}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}.

By using the above estimates, one can deduce (
3.56
) from a proper linear combination of (
3.45
) and (
3.55
).
∎

3.7.
The a priori estimates

Based on Proposition
3.4
and
3.6
, we are ready to construct the a priori estimates

(3.58)

\sup_{0<t\leq T}\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}\leq\overline{M}

where
\overline{M}\leq\min\{M_{1},M_{2}\}
is a sufficiently small positive constant.

One has

(3.59)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim 0,

which gives that

(3.60)

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}](t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}](0)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(0)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(0)\lesssim Y^{2}_{f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}}(0)

where
Y_{f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}}(0)
is defined in (
2.20
),
thus we close the a priori estimates if the initial data
Y_{f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}}(0)
is taken as small sufficiently.

Furthermore, we can get from Proposition
3.5

(3.61)

\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim Y^{2}_{f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}}(0)(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)},\quad 0\leq t\leq T.

4.
The cutoff VMB system

4.1.
Lyapunov inequality for the energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)

Proposition 4.1
.

There exist an energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)
and the corresponding energy dissipation functional
\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)
which satisfy (
2.1
), (
2.2
) respectively such that

(4.1)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{N}_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{N-1}_{x}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq N-1}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

Proof.

This proposition can be proved by a similar way as to deduce (
3.12
), we omit its proof for brevity. ∎

4.2.
The bound for energy functionals
\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)
and
\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)

Assumption I:

(4.2)

\displaystyle\sup_{0<t\leq T}\left\{\sum_{k\leq N_{0}-2}(1+t)^{k+\varrho}\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)+(1+t)^{1+\epsilon_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}\leq\mathcal{M}_{1}

where
\mathcal{M}_{1}
is a sufficiently small positive constant.

Proposition 4.2
.

Under
Assumption 1
,
assume
\tilde{\ell}\geq\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{N-1,N-1}
,
\theta=\frac{3}{\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}}
and
\ell_{1}\geq l_{1}+\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}
, then one has

(4.3)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+{\mathcal{M}_{1}}^{\frac{2}{\theta}}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t).

Proof.

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
into (
3.20
), integrating the result identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by multiplying
\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}
, then we have

(4.4)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}v\cdot E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}+q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

The coercivity estimates on the linear operator
\mathscr{L}
yields that

(4.5)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

As for the transport term on the right-hand side of (
4.4
), one has

(4.6)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}-|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}dvdx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2},

where we used the fact

\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)=\langle v\rangle^{-1}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i}).

Now we deal with the nonlinear term induced by magnetic field
B(t,x)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\right]\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\left[\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right),

when
|\alpha_{1}|=1,\beta_{1}=0
,
we apply Sobolev inequalities, interpolation method and Young inequalites to get

(4.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\times\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}\left\{\left\{\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\right\}^{\theta}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\right\}^{1-\theta}

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2},

where we take
\theta
as

\theta=\frac{3}{\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}}

and use the fact that

\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\leq\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-e_{i},\beta+e_{i}),\ |\alpha_{1}|=1,\beta_{1}=0.

When
|\alpha_{1}|=2,\beta_{1}=0
or
|\alpha_{1}|=3,\beta_{1}=0
, one has the similar bound

(4.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=2,3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=2,3}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

While for
4\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2
, since

\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\leq\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i}),

one deduces that

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\times\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim
