\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

If
|\alpha_{1}|=N-1
, one also has that the term can be bounded by

\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Thus, one has

(4.9)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1,2,3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}_{\varepsilon}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t).

By using the similar argument, one also has

(4.10)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)

and

(4.11)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t).

For the last term, one has

(4.12)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\lesssim\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t).

Now by collecting the above related estimates into (
4.4
), we arrive at

(4.13)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta)\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{2}{\theta}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,{l_{1}}}(t)

\displaystyle+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+{\mathcal{M}_{1}}^{\frac{2}{\theta}}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).

Where we used the fact that, if
\ell_{1}\geq l_{1}+\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}
, then

\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})=\langle v\rangle^{\tilde{\ell}+{l_{1}}-|\alpha|-2|\beta|}\leq\langle v\rangle^{\ell_{1}-|\alpha|-\frac{1}{2}|\beta|}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

A proper linear combination (
4.13
) with
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N,|\alpha|\leq N-1
implies (
4.14
).
∎

For later use, we also need the following result:

Proposition 4.3
.

Under
Assumption I
,
assume
\tilde{\ell}\geq\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{N_{0}-1,N_{0}-1}
,
\theta=\frac{3}{\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}}
and
l_{0}\geq l+\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}
, then one has

(4.14)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N_{0}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+{\mathcal{M}_{1}}^{\frac{2}{\theta}}\sum_{n\leq N_{0}-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{0}}(t).

Proof.

The proof is similar to Proposition
4.3
, we omit its proof for brevity.
∎

4.3.
Lyapunov inequality for the energy functionals
\mathbb{E}^{(n)}_{\ell}(t)

Lemma 4.1
.

Take
\overline{\ell}_{0}\geq\ell_{1}+\frac{3}{2}N
, for
|\alpha|=N
, it holds that

(4.15)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{\vartheta q}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{N-1\leq n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,0}}\mathcal{D}^{n,0}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

The proofs of this lemma is similar to Lemma
3.3
. For the sake of brevity, let’s just point out the differences from Lemma
3.3
.

Recalling the definition of the weight
\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)(t,v)
, one has

\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)=\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-e_{i},e_{i})\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-e_{i},e_{i}),

it follows that

(4.16)

\displaystyle{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}-2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}-1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}+1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{0}+2\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}-2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}-1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}+1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{0}+2\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}-2}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}-1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{3}_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{2-|\alpha_{1}|}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{0}+2\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}w_{\ell}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{0}+2\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\left|\left([v\times\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\eta\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{N-1\leq n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

where the third term on the right-hand side of the above inequalities can dominated by

\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{\vartheta q}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

By combining the other similar estimates as Lemma
3.3
, we complete the proof of Lemma
4.1
.
∎

Lemma 4.2
.

Assume
\overline{\ell}_{0}\geq\ell_{1}+\frac{3}{2}N
, for
{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,|\beta|\geq 1}
, one also has

(4.17)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|}_{\ell_{1}}(t).

Proof.

To this end, applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
into (
3.20
), multiplying
\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}
and integrating the result identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, then we have

(4.18)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}v\cdot E_{\varepsilon}f_{\varepsilon}+q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

The coercivity estimates on the linear operator
\mathscr{L}
yields that

(4.19)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

As for the transport term on the right-hand side of (
4.18
), one has

(4.20)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}dvdx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2},

where we used the fact

\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)=\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i}).

For the nonlinear term induced by magnetic field
B(t,x)
, simiar to (
4.16
), one has

(4.21)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\right]\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\left[\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}^{2}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Similarly, one also has

(4.22)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[q_{0}E\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|E_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

The last term can be dominated by

(4.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

(
4.17
) follows by collecting the above estimates into (
4.18
), thus we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

A proper linear combination of Lemma
4.1
.
and Lemma
4.2
, one has

Proposition 4.4
.

Under
Assumption 1
and take

(4.24)

\sigma_{N,0}=\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2},\ \sigma_{N,|\beta|}-\sigma_{N,|\beta|-1}=\frac{1+\vartheta}{2},|\beta|\geq 1,

where
0<\epsilon_{0}\ll 1
,
one can deduce that

(4.25)

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathbb{E}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta{\varepsilon^{2}}(1+t)^{-2\sigma_{N,0}}\left\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{1}\left\{(1+t)^{-1^{+}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)\right\}.

Proof.

To control the singularty term

\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

on the righ-hand side of (
4.15
), we firstly multiply
\varepsilon^{2}
into (
4.15
), then one has

(4.26)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|w_{\ell}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|w_{\ell}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{\vartheta q\varepsilon^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle w_{\ell}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{N}_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t).

When
|\alpha|=N-1,\ |\beta|=1
, we hope that the term

\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

on the right-hand side of (
4.17
) can be dominated by the corresponding dissipation terms on the left-hand side of (
4.26
), to do so, we multiply
\varepsilon^{2}
into (
4.17
) to get

(4.27)

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon(1+t)^{\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{N+1-|\alpha_{1}|,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{3\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{0}+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}^{n,|\beta|+1}_{\ell_{1}}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t).

Furthermore, to control the time increasing rates
(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}
for the first term on the right-hand side of (
4.27
), we multiply
(1+t)^{-\sigma_{|\alpha|+|\beta|,|\beta|}}
into (
4.27
) and (
4.26
), and take a proper linear combination of the result equalities, then we have

(4.28)

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\sum_{|\alpha|=N}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\left\{\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{\vartheta q{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\left\{\left\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{q\vartheta{\varepsilon^{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|\widetilde{w}_{\ell_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\varepsilon^{2}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\left\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|{M}^{\delta}\nabla^{N}_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{1}\left\{(1+t)^{-(1+\epsilon_{0})}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta{\varepsilon^{2}}(1+t)^{-2\sigma_{N,0}}\left\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{1}\left\{(1+t)^{-(1+\epsilon_{0})}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)\right\}.

where we use (
4.24
).
∎

Proposition 4.5
.

For
N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1
, it holds that

(4.29)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{1}}^{(n)}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}_{\ell_{1}}^{(n)}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{M}_{1}\left\{(1+t)^{-(1+\epsilon_{0})}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)\right\}.

Proof.

This propositon can be proved by a similar way as Proposition
4.4
, we omit its proof for brevity.
∎

When
n\leq N_{0}
, one also has

Proposition 4.6
.

For any
\ell_{0}\geq N_{0}
, it holds that

(4.30)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{0}}^{(n)}(t)+\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{D}_{\ell_{0}}^{(n)}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{N_{0}+1}(t)+\mathcal{M}_{1}\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{D}^{(n)}_{\ell_{0}}(t).

4.4.
The temporal time decay rates for

\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)
and
\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)

To this end, we need

Assumption II:

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\bar{\ell}_{0}+\frac{5}{2}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\right\}\leq\mathcal{M}_{2},

where
\mathcal{M}_{2}
is a sufficiently small positive constant.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under
Assumption I
and
Assumption II
, there exists suitably large constants
\bar{l}
, and take
l\geq\bar{l}
,
\widetilde{k}=\min\{k+1,N_{0}-1\}
let
N_{0}\geq 4
,
N=2N_{0}
, one has the following estimates:

(i).

For
k=0,1,\cdots,N_{0}-1
, it holds that

(4.31)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\nabla^{k}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right)+\eta\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

(iii).

For
k=N_{0}
, it follows that

(4.32)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}-1}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\right)+\eta\left\|\nabla^{N_{0}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

(iii).

For
k=0,1,2\cdots,N_{0}-1
, there exist interactive energy functionals
G^{k}_{f}(t)
satisfying

G^{k}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\nabla^{k}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+1}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+2}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

such that

(4.33)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}G^{k}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)+\left\|\nabla^{k}[E_{\varepsilon},\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho^{-}_{\varepsilon}]\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k+1}[{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}])\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{\widetilde{k}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}\right)+\frac{1}{{\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}.

Proof.

We can use the method of proving Lemma 3.4-3.5 in
[
61
]
to prove this lemma. For the sake of brevity, we only provide some distinct and crucial estimates here, and similar calculations can be carried out for other related estimates.

For the case
1\leq j\leq k-1\leq N_{0}-2
, we can control

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{x}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla^{j}_{x}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}]\cdot\nabla_{v}\nabla^{k-j}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\nabla^{k}_{x}{{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}}dxdv,

by using interpolation with respect to both spatial-velocity derivatives and velocity. Take
\varrho=\frac{1}{2}
for brevity,
when
j=1
, the estimate is given as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{x}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{v}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}[v\times B_{\varepsilon}]\cdot\nabla_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\nabla^{k}_{x}{{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}}dxdv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{k}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-{\frac{1}{2}}}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k-\frac{3}{2}}{k+\frac{2}{3}}}\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{m_{1}+1}_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{m_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\Lambda^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{1}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-{\frac{1}{2}}}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k-\frac{3}{2}}{k+\frac{2}{3}}}\left\|\nabla^{m_{1}+1}_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{m_{1}+1}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{1}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-{\frac{1}{2}}}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k-\frac{3}{2}}{k+\frac{2}{3}}}\left\|\nabla^{m_{1}+1}_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{m_{1}+1}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{1}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}\times\frac{\bar{l}_{1}}{\bar{l}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\bar{l}_{1}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}\times\frac{1}{\bar{l}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{\bar{l}_{2}-1}{\bar{l}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\bar{l}_{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\bar{l}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-{\frac{1}{2}}}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k-\frac{3}{2}}{k+\frac{2}{3}}}\left\|\nabla^{m_{1}+1}_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{m_{1}+1}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{1}{k+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\bar{l}_{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\bar{l}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\bar{l}_{1}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}\times\frac{1}{\bar{l}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}\times\frac{\bar{l}_{1}}{\bar{l}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{\bar{l}_{2}-1}{\bar{l}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\max{\{\bar{l}_{1},\bar{l}_{2}\}}}\nabla^{k}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|,\left\|\nabla^{m_{1}+1}_{v}\nabla^{k-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{m_{1}+1}},\left\|\Lambda^{-{\frac{1}{2}}}[f,B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left(\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}\right)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}\left(\left\|\nabla^{k+1}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}\right)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}.

In fact, we can take proper
m_{1}
, which satisfies

m_{1}>\frac{k}{2}-1-\frac{3}{8k},\ m_{1}+k\leq 2N_{0}-1

such that

\ \frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}>1,

so obviously there exists suitably large constants
\bar{l}_{1}
and
\bar{l}_{2}
such that the following equation holds

\frac{3}{k+\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{m_{1}}{m_{1}+1}\times\frac{k-\frac{1}{2}}{k+\frac{1}{2}}\times\frac{\bar{l}_{1}}{\bar{l}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\bar{l}_{2}-1}{\bar{l}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}=2.

Here we ask
\bar{l}\geq\max{\{\bar{l}_{1},\bar{l}_{2}\}}
.
Based on the aforementioned estimated ideas, we can prove (
4.31
), (
4.32
) and (
4.33
), thus the proof of this lemma is complete.
∎

Proposition 4.7
.

Under
Assumption I
and
Assumption II
, assume
N_{0}\geq 5
,
N=2N_{0}
,
there exist an energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)
and the corresponding energy dissipation rate functional
\mathcal{D}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)
satisfying (
2.6
) and (
2.7
) respectively such that

(4.34)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\leq 0

holds for
k=0,1,2,\cdots,N_{0}-2
and all
0\leq t\leq T.

Furthermore, we can get that

(4.35)

\mathcal{E}_{k\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim\min\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)}.

Proof.

From (
4.31
), (
4.31
) and (
4.33
), one has (
4.34
).
The decay estimtates (
4.35
) can be proved by a similar way as Proposition
3.5
, we omit its proof for brevity.
∎

Besides, we need to deduce the temporal time decay rates for
\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)
.

Proposition 4.8
.

Under
Assumption I
and
Assumption II
,
it holds that

(4.36)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\right\}+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim 0.

Furthermore, one also deduces that

(4.37)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim\min\{\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}\}(1+t)^{-1-\varrho}.

Proof.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)
in (
2.18
),
applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
into (
3.20
), integrating the result identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by multiplying
\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}
with
|\alpha|\geq 1
, then we have

(4.38)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v{M}^{1/2}q_{1}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon})-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[q_{0}v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right]\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}\left[\tfrac{1}{2}q_{0}(E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v)f_{\varepsilon}-q_{0}E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right).

The coercivity estimates on the linear operator
\mathscr{L}
yields that

(4.39)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{L}f_{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

As for the transport term on the right-hand side of (
4.38
), since

\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)=\langle v\rangle^{-1}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i}),

one has

(4.40)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}dvdx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\left\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}.

The second and third terms on the right-hand side of (
4.38
) can be dominated by

(4.41)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}_{x}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{|\alpha|\rightarrow N_{0}}(t).

For the nonlinear term induced by magnetic field
B(t,x)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\right]\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\left[\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right),

when
|\alpha_{1}|=1,\beta_{1}=0
,
we apply Sobolev inequalities, interpolation method and Young inequalites to get

(4.42)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\times\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha-\alpha_{1},\beta+e_{i})\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{5}{2}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

The other cases have similar bound as (
4.42
). Consequently, one has

(4.43)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\times B_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{5}{2}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

By using the similar argument, one also has

(4.44)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{3}{2}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

and

(4.45)

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[E_{\varepsilon}\cdot v\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right],\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{3}{2}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2},

one has

(4.46)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}(f_{\varepsilon},f_{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+{\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f_{\varepsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Now by collecting the above related estimates into (
4.38
), we arrive at

(4.47)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{w}_{\overline{\ell}_{0}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{5}{2}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{|\alpha|\rightarrow N_{0}}(t),

Taking summation for (
4.47
) with
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{0}-1,|\alpha|\geq 1
, one has

(4.48)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)

which combining with (
4.34
) yields that

(4.49)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\right\}+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}-1,\overline{\ell}_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{1\rightarrow N_{0}}(t)\lesssim 0.

(
4.37
) follows by a similar way as (
4.35
), thus we complete the proof of this Propostion
4.8
.
∎

4.5.
The bound for
\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}](t)\|^{2}

To ensure
Assumption 2
, one also need the following result:

Proposition 4.9
.

Under Under
Assumption 1
and
Assumption 2
, one has

(4.50)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}G^{-\varrho}_{f_{\varepsilon}}(t)\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}{\bf P}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}[E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\kappa_{1}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{+}-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-})\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}.

Proof.

This proposition can be proved by a similar way as Proposition
3.6
, we omit its proof for brevity.
∎

4.6.
The a priori estimates

Now we are ready to construct the a priori estimates:

(4.51)

\displaystyle X(T)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{0}}^{(n)}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{1}}^{(n)}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{1}}^{(N)}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\right\}\leq\mathcal{M}.

Proposition 4.10
.

Assume that

•

N_{0}\geq 5
,
N=2N_{0}
;

•

-1\leq\gamma<0
,
\frac{1}{2}\leq\varrho<\frac{3}{2}
,
0<\vartheta\leq\frac{2}{3}\rho
;

•

0<\epsilon_{0}\leq 2(1+\varrho)
;

•

\sigma_{N,0}=\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}
,
\sigma_{n,0}=0
for
n\leq N-1
,
\sigma_{n,j+1}-\sigma_{n,j}=\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}
;

•

\bar{l}
is a sufficiently large positive constant;

•

l_{1}\geq N+\bar{l}
,
\tilde{\ell}\geq\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{N-1,N-1}
,
\ell_{1}\geq l_{1}+\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}
,
\overline{\ell}_{0}\geq\ell_{1}+\frac{3}{2}N
,
l_{0}\geq\overline{\ell}_{0}+\frac{5}{2}
,
\ell_{0}\geq l_{0}+\tilde{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}
,

under the a priori estimates (
4.51
), we can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{0}}^{(n)}(t)+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{1}}^{(n)}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\ell_{1}}^{(N)}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}[f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\right\}+\sum_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathbb{D}_{\ell_{0}}^{(n)}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{0}+1\leq n\leq N-1}\mathbb{D}_{\ell_{1}}^{(n)}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathbb{D}_{\ell_{1}}^{(N)}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N_{0}-1,l_{0}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\lesssim 0

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

Recalling the definition of
\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell}(t)
and
\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l}(t)
, (
4.1
) tells us that

(4.52)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{N}_{x}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}B_{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,1}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,1}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{N-1}_{x}\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq N-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).

By multiplying
(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}
into (
4.52
), one has

(4.53)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\{(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\right\}+\epsilon_{0}(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)+(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathbb{D}^{(N)}_{\ell_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).

Thus (
4.10
) follows from (
4.52
), (
4.53
), (
4.25
), (
4.29
), (
4.30
) and (
4.50
), which complete the proof of this proposition.
∎

5.
Limit to two fluid incompressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier-Maxwell equations with Ohm’s law

In this section, we will derive the two fluid incompressible NSFM equations (
1.15
) with Ohm’s law from the perturbed two-species VMB as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
.

As
[
53
]
, we first introduce the following fluid variables

(5.1)

\displaystyle\rho_{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle{f}_{\varepsilon},{q_{2}}\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\ u_{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle f_{\varepsilon},{q_{2}}v\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\ \theta_{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle f_{\varepsilon},{q_{2}}(\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{3}-1)\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,

\displaystyle n_{\varepsilon}=\langle f_{\varepsilon},{q_{1}}\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\ j_{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\langle f_{\varepsilon},{q_{1}}v\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\ w_{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\langle f_{\varepsilon},{q_{1}}(\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{3}-1)\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

We use the similar argument as (
A.18
), we can deduce the following local conservation laws

(5.2)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}\rho_{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathrm{div}_{x}\,u_{\varepsilon}=0\,,\\
\partial_{t}u_{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}(\rho_{\varepsilon}+\theta_{\varepsilon})+\mathrm{div}_{x}\,\big\langle\widehat{A}(v)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}},\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L}(\tfrac{f_{\varepsilon}}{2})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\tfrac{1}{2}(n_{\varepsilon}E_{\varepsilon}+j_{\varepsilon}\times B_{\varepsilon})\,,\\
\partial_{t}\theta_{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{2}{3}\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathrm{div}_{x}\,u_{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\,\big\langle\widehat{B}(v)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}},\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathscr{L}(\tfrac{f_{\varepsilon}}{2})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\tfrac{\varepsilon}{3}j_{\varepsilon}\cdot E_{\varepsilon}\,,\\
\partial_{t}n_{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{div}_{x}\,j_{\varepsilon}=0\,,\\
\partial_{t}E_{\varepsilon}-\nabla_{x}\times B_{\varepsilon}=-j_{\varepsilon}\,,\\
\partial_{t}B_{\varepsilon}+\nabla_{x}\times E_{\varepsilon}=0\,,\\
\mathrm{div}_{x}\,E_{\varepsilon}=n_{\varepsilon}\,,\quad\mathrm{div}_{x}B_{\varepsilon}=0\,.\end{array}\right.

where
\mathscr{L}[\widehat{A}(v)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}}]=\left(v\otimes v-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}I_{3}\right)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}}\in\ker^{\bot}({\mathscr{L}})
with
\widehat{A}(v)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}}\in\ker^{\bot}({\mathscr{L}})\cdot{q_{2}}

and
\mathscr{L}[\widehat{B}(v)\sqrt{M}]=\left(v\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}-\frac{5}{2}\right)\right)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}}\in\ker^{\bot}({\mathscr{L}})
with
\widehat{B}(v)\sqrt{M}\cdot{q_{2}}\in\ker^{\bot}({\mathscr{L}})
.

Based on Theorem
2.1
and Theorem
2.2
, the Cauchy problem to (
1.12
) admits a global solution

(f_{\epsilon},E_{\epsilon},B_{\epsilon})
belonging to
L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+};H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v})
, for the noncutoff cases, from (
2.9
), one has

(5.3)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\leq\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(0)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(0)\leq C

and

(5.4)

\displaystyle\int^{\infty}_{0}\left\{\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N-1,l}(t)\right\}dt\leq C

where
C
is independent of
\varepsilon
. In fact, (
5.4
) tells us that

(5.5)

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\int^{\infty}_{0}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{D}dt\lesssim C\varepsilon^{2}.

which yields that

(5.6)

\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\rightarrow 0,\textrm{\ strongly in $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+};H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{D})$ as $\varepsilon\rightarrow 0$.}

As for the cutoff cases, from (
2.2
), one also deduces that

(5.7)

\{{\bf I-P}\}f_{\varepsilon}\rightarrow 0,\textrm{\ strongly in $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+};H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu})$ as $\varepsilon\rightarrow 0$.}

By standard convergent method, there exist
f,E,B,\rho,u,\theta,n,w,j
such that

\displaystyle f_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle f,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}$},

\displaystyle E_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle E,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle B_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle B,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle\rho_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\rho,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle u_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle u,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle\theta_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\theta,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle n_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle n,\textrm{weakly$-*$ for $t>0$, weakly in $H^{N}_{x}$},

\displaystyle w_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle w,\textrm{weakly in $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v})$},

(5.8)

\displaystyle j_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle j,\textrm{weakly in $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v})$}.

where

(5.9)

\displaystyle f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\rho+n/2\right)\frac{q_{1}+q_{2}}{2}{M}^{1/2}+\left(\rho-n/2\right)\frac{q_{1}-q_{2}}{2}{M}^{1/2}

\displaystyle+u\cdot vq_{2}{{M}}^{1/2}+\theta q_{2}(|v|^{2}-3){M}^{1/2}

with

(5.10)

\displaystyle\rho=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle{f},{q_{2}}\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,\ u=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle f,{q_{2}}v\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,

\displaystyle\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\langle f,{q_{2}}(\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{3}-1)\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,,n=\langle f,{q_{1}}\sqrt{M}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,.

In the sense of distributions, utilizing the uniform estimates (
5.3
), (
5.4
) and (
5.5
), applying Aubin-Lions-Simon Theorem and the similar argument as
[
53
]
, we can deduce that

(u,\theta,n,E,B)\in C(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N-1}_{x})\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N}_{x})

satisfy the following two fluid incompressible NSFM equations with Ohm’s law

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}u+u\cdot\nabla_{x}u-\mu\Delta_{x}u+\nabla_{x}p=\tfrac{1}{2}(nE+j\times B)\,,\qquad\mathrm{div}_{x}\,u=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}\theta+u\cdot\nabla_{x}\theta-\kappa\Delta_{x}\theta=0\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\ \,\rho+\theta=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}E-\nabla_{x}\times B=-j\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\ \ \ \,\mathrm{div}_{x}\,E=n\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}B+\nabla_{x}\times E=0\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathrm{div}_{x}\,B=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\qquad\qquad j-nu=\sigma\big(-\tfrac{1}{2}\nabla_{x}n+E+u\times B\big)\,,\qquad\quad\,\ w=\tfrac{3}{2}n\theta\,,\end{array}\right.

with initial data

\displaystyle u(0,x)=\mathcal{P}u^{in}(x)\,,\ \theta(0,x)=\tfrac{3}{5}\theta^{in}(x)-\tfrac{2}{5}\rho^{in}(x)\,,\ E(0,x)=E^{in}(x)\,,\ B(0,x)=B^{in}(x)\,.

We omit its detail proof for brevity.
Moreover, from the uniform bound (
2.9
) in Theorem
2.1
, (
2.2
) in Theorem
2.2
and the convergence (
5
), we have

(5.11)

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\big(\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\big(\|f_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|B_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle Y^{2}_{f_{\varepsilon},E_{\varepsilon},B_{\varepsilon}}(0)\rightarrow Y^{2}_{f,E,B}(0)

as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
. Hence

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\big(\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)(t)\lesssim Y^{2}_{f,E,B}(0).

Recalling the definition of
f
in (
5.9
), there are positive generic constants
C_{h}
and
C_{l}
such that

\displaystyle C_{l}\big(\|u\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|n\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)\leq\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C_{h}\big(\|u\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|n\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)\,.

Consequently, the solution
(u,\theta,n,E,B)
to the two fluid incompressible NSFM equations (
1.15
) with Ohm’s law constructed above admits the energy bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\big(

\displaystyle\|u\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|n\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|E\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|B\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\big)(t)\lesssim Y^{2}_{f,E,B}(0).

Then the proof of Theorem
2.3
is complete.

Appendix A
Appendix

A.1.
Properties of the collision operator for non-cutoff cases

Take

w_{\ell,\vartheta}=\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}.

Lemma A.1
.

Let
\ell\in\mathbb{R}
,
\eta>0
,
0<s<1
and
\max\{-3,-\frac{3}{2}-2s\}<\gamma<0
.

(i).

It holds that

(A.1)

\langle\mathscr{L}g,g\rangle\gtrsim|\{{\bf{I}}-{\bf{P}}\}g|_{L^{2}_{D}}^{2}.

(ii).

It holds that

(A.2)

\left\langle w_{\ell,\vartheta}^{2}\mathscr{L}g,g\right\rangle\gtrsim|w_{\ell,\vartheta}g|_{D}^{2}-C|g|^{2}_{L^{2}_{B_{C}}}.

For
|\beta|\geq 1
, one has

(A.3)

\left\langle w_{\ell,\vartheta}^{2}\partial_{\beta}\mathscr{L}g,\partial_{\beta}g\right\rangle\gtrsim\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}g\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}^{2}-C\sum_{\beta^{\prime}<\beta}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}^{2}-C|g|^{2}_{L^{2}_{B_{C}}}.

Proof.

(
A.1
) has been shown in
[
1
]
.
The relevant coercive estimate (
A.2
) and (
A.3
) with exponential weights can be found in
[
23
]
.

∎

Lemma A.2
.

For all
0<s<1
,
{q}>0
and
\ell\geq 0
,

i)

It holds that

(A.4)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f,g),w_{\ell,\vartheta}^{2}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\left\{\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}+\left|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}\right\}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\left|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},\left|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\right\}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{L^{2}_{D}}

\displaystyle+\sum\left|e^{\frac{{q}\langle v\rangle}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{L^{2}_{D}},

where the summation
\sum
is taken over
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha
and
\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta
.

Furthermore, we have

(A.5)

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathscr{T}(f,g),h\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{|f|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}|g|_{L^{2}_{D}}+|g|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}|f|_{L^{2}_{D}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\min\left\{|f|_{L^{2}_{v}}|g|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},|g|_{L^{2}}|f|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\right\}\right\}|h|_{L^{2}_{D}}.

ii)

For
0<s<1,\ m\geq 4,\ \ell>0
, it holds that

(A.6)

\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f,f)\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}f\right|_{H^{3}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}f\right|_{H^{1}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},

or

(A.7)

\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}\mathscr{T}(f,f)\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|w_{\ell,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}f\right|_{H^{4}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}.

Proof.

Actually, for the case of weak angular singularity
0<s<\frac{1}{2}
, (
A.4
) is shown as in
[
28
]
. For the case of strong angular singularity
\frac{1}{2}\leq s<1
, we can follow the proof strategy of
[
28
]
and
[
23
]
with slight modifications to obtain (
A.4
). For the sake of brevity, we omit the detailed proof. (
A.5
) and (
A.6
)-(
A.7
) have been shown in
[
1
]
and
[
23
]
.
∎

A.2.
Properties of the collision operator for angular cutoff cases

Take

w_{l,\vartheta}=\langle v\rangle^{l}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}.

Lemma A.3
.

Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,

i)

One has

(A.8)

\langle\mathscr{L}g,g\rangle\geq|{\bf\{I-P\}}g|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}.

ii)

It holds that

(A.9)

\left\langle w_{l,\vartheta}^{2}\mathscr{L}g,g\right\rangle\gtrsim|w_{l,\vartheta}g|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}^{2}-C\|g\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{B_{C}}}.

For
|\beta|\geq 1
, one has

(A.10)

\left\langle w_{l,\vartheta}^{2}\partial_{\beta}\mathscr{L}g,\partial_{\beta}g\right\rangle\gtrsim\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}^{2}-C\sum_{\beta^{\prime}<\beta}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}^{2}-C\|g\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{B_{C}}}.

Proof.

The detail proof of this lemma can be found in
[
39
]
and
[
24
]
.
∎

Lemma A.4
.

Let
-3<\gamma<0
,
N\geq 4
, one has

(A.11)

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathscr{T}_{\pm}(g_{1},g_{2})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum C_{\beta}^{\beta_{0}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\mathscr{T}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum C_{\beta}^{\beta_{0}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-u|^{\gamma}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\partial_{\beta_{0}}[\mu(u)^{\frac{1}{2}}]\left\{\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\pm}(u^{\prime})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\mp}(u^{\prime})-\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v)\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\pm}(u)-\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v)\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\mp}(u)\right\}d\omega du,

where
g_{i}(t,x,v)=[g_{i+}(t,x,v),g_{i-}(t,x,v)]

(i=1,2)
and the summations are taken for all
\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta,\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha
.

(i).

When
|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|\leq N
, we have

(A.12)

\begin{split}&\left\langle w_{l,\vartheta}^{2}\mathscr{T}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right\rangle\\
\lesssim&\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right\}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left|e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\\
&+\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right\}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\left|e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}},\end{split}

or

(A.13)

\begin{split}&\left\langle w_{l,\vartheta}^{2}\mathscr{T}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right\rangle\\
\lesssim&\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right\}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left|e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\\
&+\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right\}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\left|e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{l,\vartheta}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}.\end{split}

(ii).

Set
\varsigma(v)=\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}\equiv\nu(v)^{-1},~l\geq 0
, it holds that

(A.14)

\begin{split}\left|\varsigma^{l}\mathscr{T}(g_{1},g_{2})\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}&\lesssim\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\left|\varsigma^{l-|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}g_{1}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|\varsigma^{l}g_{2}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}},\\
\left|\varsigma^{l}\mathscr{T}(g_{1},g_{2})\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}&\lesssim\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\left|\varsigma^{l}g_{1}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|\varsigma^{l-|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}g_{2}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}.\end{split}

Proof.

The detail proof of this lemma can be found in
[
24
]
.
∎

A.3.
The proof of Lemma
3.2

Proof.

Note that the proof here is not only valid for the non-cutoff cases, but also for the grad cutoff cases. For the sake of convenience, we use
\|\cdot\|_{D\vee\nu}
to denote the dissipative norm
\|\cdot\|_{D}
under non-cutoff cases or
\|\cdot\|_{\nu}
under cutoff cases.

By applying the macro-micro decomposition
(\ref{macro-micro})
introduced in
[
40
]
and by defining moment functions
\mathcal{A}_{mj}(f_{\varepsilon})
and
\mathcal{B}_{j}(f_{\varepsilon}),~1\leq m,j\leq 3,
by

\mathcal{A}_{mj}(f_{\varepsilon})=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(v_{m}v_{j}-1\right){M}^{1/2}f_{\varepsilon}dv,\quad\mathcal{B}_{j}(f_{\varepsilon})=\frac{1}{10}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|v|^{2}-5\right)v_{j}{M}^{1/2}f_{\varepsilon}dv,

one can then derive from
(\ref{VMB-F-perturbative})
a fluid-type system of equations

(A.15)

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\cdot u_{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v{M}^{1/2},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=\left\langle{M}^{1/2},g_{\pm}\right\rangle,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}\left(u_{\varepsilon,i}+\left\langle v_{i}{M}^{1/2},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial_{i}\left(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}+2\theta_{\varepsilon}\right)\mp\frac{1}{\varepsilon}E_{\varepsilon,i}\\[5.69054pt]
\quad\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle vv_{i}{M}^{1/2},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=\left\langle v_{i}{M}^{1/2},g_{\pm}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathscr{L}_{\pm}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle,\\[5.69054pt]
\partial_{t}\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{6}\left\langle(|v|^{2}-3){M}^{1/2},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{3\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\cdot u_{\varepsilon}\\[5.69054pt]
\quad\quad+\frac{1}{6\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\left\langle(|v|^{2}-3)v{M}^{1/2},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=\left\langle(|v|^{2}-3){M}^{1/2},g_{\pm}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\mathscr{L}_{\pm}f_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle,\end{cases}

and
